\mathsf{inr}{\,V}\mapsto\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\Delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(N[V/x]))\right)\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}L>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\begin{cases}\mathsf{inl}{\,V}\mapsto\Delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M[V/x])\right))\\
\mathsf{inr}{\,V}\mapsto\Delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(N[V/x])\right))\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}L>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\begin{cases}\mathsf{inl}{\,V}\mapsto(\Delta^{\kappa}\llparenthesis{M[V/x]}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa})\\
\mathsf{inr}{\,V}\mapsto(\Delta^{\kappa}\llparenthesis{N[V/x]}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa})\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}L>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\begin{cases}\mathsf{inl}{\,V}\mapsto\Delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\llparenthesis{V}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}})\\
\mathsf{inr}{\,V}\mapsto\Delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\llparenthesis{V}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}})\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}L>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda V.\mathsf{match\ }\llparenthesis{V}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\mathsf{\ with}\begin{cases}{{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{v}\mapsto(\Delta^{\kappa}\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{v})\\
{{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{v}\mapsto(\Delta^{\kappa}\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{v})\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\left(\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}L)\right)>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\begin{cases}{{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{v}\mapsto\Delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{v})\\
{{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{v}\mapsto\Delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{v})\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\llparenthesis{L}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\begin{cases}{{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{v}\mapsto\Delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{v})\\
{{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{v}\mapsto\Delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{v})\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\llparenthesis{\mathsf{case}(L,x.M,y.N)}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}

Case
:
M=\mathsf{fold}{\,M^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{fold}{\,M^{\prime}})\right)=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left((\mathsf{fold}{\,})\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{\mathsf{fold}{\,-}}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}\circ\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}\right)\left(\mathcal{D}{(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa})}\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}\right)\left(\llparenthesis{M^{\prime}}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\llparenthesis{\mathsf{fold}{\,M^{\prime}}}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}

Case
:
M=\mathsf{unfold}{\,M^{\prime}}
.
Note that for
V^{\prime}:\mathsf{Val}_{\mathsf{\tau[\mu{X}.{\tau}/X]}}
we have that

{\triangleright}\,(\alpha\!:\!\kappa).\left(\llparenthesis{\mathsf{fold}{\,V^{\prime}}}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\,[\alpha]=\llparenthesis{V^{\prime}}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)

and thus we get

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{unfold}{\,M}^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda(\mathsf{fold}{\,V^{\prime}}).\Delta^{\kappa}(\delta^{\kappa}(V^{\prime}))\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda(\mathsf{fold}{\,V^{\prime}}).\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)(\Delta^{\kappa}(\delta^{\kappa}(V^{\prime})))

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda(\mathsf{fold}{\,V^{\prime}}).(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax{\alpha}{\kappa}(\delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{V^{\prime}}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}))))

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda(\mathsf{fold}{\,V^{\prime}}).(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax{\alpha}{\kappa}(\delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{\mathsf{fold}{\,V^{\prime}}}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\,[\alpha]))))

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda V.(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax{\alpha}{\kappa}(\delta^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{V}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\,[\alpha]))))

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\left(\lambda v.\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax{\alpha}{\kappa}\delta^{\kappa}(v\,[\alpha]))\right)\circ\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle(\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}(M^{\prime})))>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda v.\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax{\alpha}{\kappa}\delta^{\kappa}(v\,[\alpha]{}))

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\llparenthesis{\mathsf{unfold}{\,M}^{\prime}}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}

Case
:
M=\mathsf{choice}^{p}(N_{1},N_{2})

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}{}(\mathsf{choice}^{p}(N_{1},N_{2}))\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left({\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}{}(N_{1})}\oplus^{\kappa}_{p}{\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}{}(N_{2})}\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle{\left(\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}{}(N_{1})\right)\right)}\oplus^{\kappa}_{p}{\left(\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}\!\!\left(\llparenthesis{-}\rrparenthesis^{\mathsf{Val},\kappa}\right)\left(\mathsf{eval}^{\kappa}{}(N_{2})\right)\right)}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle{\llparenthesis{N_{1}}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{p}{\llparenthesis{N_{2}}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\llparenthesis{\mathsf{choice}^{p}(N_{1},N_{2})}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}

∎

As a consequence, we note the following easy corollary.

Corollary A.14.

If
M
is a closed term of unit type, then

\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\mathsf{eval}{\,M})=\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis)

To relate the two denotational semantics we
construct a logical relation

\displaystyle{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma}\,{}

\displaystyle:\llparenthesis{{\sigma}}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}\to\mathsf{Prop}

\displaystyle{}\,\preceq^{\kappa,{\sf Tm}}_{\sigma}\,{}

\displaystyle:\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}{}{\llparenthesis{{\sigma}}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}}\to\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}{}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}})\to\mathsf{Prop}

by guarded recursion and induction over
\sigma
as follows

\displaystyle v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{1}\,{}w

\displaystyle\triangleq\mathsf{tt}

\displaystyle n{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\mathsf{Nat}}\,{}m

\displaystyle\triangleq(n=m)

\displaystyle v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{{\sigma}\times{\tau}}\,{}w

\displaystyle\triangleq\left(\mathsf{pr}_{1}{}v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma}\,{}\mathsf{pr}_{1}{}w\right)\land\left(\mathsf{pr}_{2}{}v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau}\,{}\mathsf{pr}_{2}{}w\right)

\displaystyle v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{{\sigma}+{\tau}}\,{}w

\displaystyle\triangleq\left(\exists v^{\prime},w^{\prime}.v={{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{v}^{\prime}\land w={{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{w}^{\prime}\land v^{\prime}{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma}\,{}w^{\prime}\right)\lor

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\left(\exists v^{\prime},w^{\prime}.v={{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{v}^{\prime}\land w={{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{w}^{\prime}\land v^{\prime}{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau}\,{}w^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle f{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma\to\tau}\,{}g

\displaystyle\triangleq\forall v,w.(v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma}\,{}w)\to(f\,v){}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{\tau}\,{}(\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa]\,(w\,[\kappa]))

\displaystyle v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\mu{X}.{\tau}}\,{}w

\displaystyle\triangleq{\triangleright}\,(\alpha\!:\!\kappa).(v\,[\alpha]{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau[\mu{X}.{\tau}/X]}\,{}\mathsf{force}(w))

\displaystyle x{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{\sigma}\,{}y

\displaystyle\triangleq x{}\,{}\overline{{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma}\,{}}^{\kappa}\,{}y

Note that many of these cases use implicit isomorphism, for example

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\mathsf{Nat}}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}

\displaystyle=\forall\kappa.\mathbb{N}\simeq\mathbb{N}

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{{\sigma}+{\tau}}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}

\displaystyle=\forall\kappa.(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}+\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})\simeq(\forall\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})+(\forall\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})=\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}+\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}

In the case of function types,
g:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma\to\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}
and so
\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa]\,(w\,[\kappa]):\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})
, which is not quite right, because,
to apply
{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{\tau}\,{}
we need something of type
\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}})
. However, the next lemma states that these sets are isomorphic, and we will leave the
isomorphism implicit in order not to clutter proofs.

Lemma A.15.

Given a type
X
, in which
\kappa
may appear, the two types
\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}(\forall\kappa.X)
and
\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(X)
are isomorphic. The isomorphism is natural in
X
.

Proof.

Define the map
\Psi:\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}(\forall\kappa.X)\to\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(X)
as
\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{ev}_{\kappa})
, so

\displaystyle\Psi(\delta^{\forall\!}{x})\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=\delta^{\kappa}{(x\,[\kappa])}

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathsf{step}^{\!\forall\!}{x})\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}{(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa\Psi(x)\,[\kappa])}

\displaystyle\Psi({x}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{p}{y})\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle={\Psi(x)\,[\kappa]}\oplus^{\kappa}_{p}{\Psi(y)\,[\kappa]}

For the other direction, use the isomorphism

(15)

\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(X)\simeq\mathcal{D}{}(\forall\kappa.X+\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(X))

to define
\Phi:(\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(X))\to\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}(\forall\kappa.X)
and
\Phi^{\kappa}:(\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(X))\to\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\forall\kappa.X)
by

\displaystyle\Phi(x)

\displaystyle\triangleq\forall\kappa.\Phi^{\kappa}(x)

\displaystyle\Phi^{\kappa}(\delta(\mathsf{inl}\,{x}))

\displaystyle\triangleq\delta^{\kappa}(x)

\displaystyle\Phi^{\kappa}(\delta(\mathsf{inr}\,{x}))

\displaystyle\triangleq\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa{\Phi^{\kappa}(x)})

\displaystyle\Phi^{\kappa}({x}\oplus_{p}{y})

\displaystyle\triangleq{\Phi^{\kappa}(x)}\oplus^{\kappa}_{p}{\Phi^{\kappa}(y)}

We show that
(\Phi\circ\Psi(x))\,[\kappa]=x\,[\kappa]
by guarded recursion and
\mathcal{D}
-induction:

\displaystyle(\Phi\circ\Psi(\delta^{\forall\!}(x)))\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=\Phi^{\kappa}(\delta(\mathsf{inl}{\,x}))

\displaystyle=\delta^{\kappa}{x}

\displaystyle=\delta^{\forall\!}x\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle(\Phi\circ\Psi(\mathsf{step}^{\!\forall\!}(x)))\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=\Phi^{\kappa}(\Psi(\mathsf{step}^{\!\forall\!}x))

\displaystyle=\Phi^{\kappa}(\delta(\mathsf{inr}\,(\Psi(x)))

\displaystyle=\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa{\Phi^{\kappa}(\Psi(x))})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa{x\,[\kappa]})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{step}^{\!\forall\!}x\,[\kappa]

The case of
{x}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{p}{y}
is also easy. Similarly, we show that
\Psi(\Phi(x))\,[\kappa]=x\,[\kappa]
by guarded recursion:

\displaystyle\Psi(\Phi(\delta(\mathsf{inl}\,{x})))\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=\Psi(\delta^{\forall\!}(x))\,[\kappa]=\delta^{\kappa}{(x\,[\kappa])}

which up to the isomorphism (
15
) is
\delta(\mathsf{inl}\,{x})
.

\displaystyle\Psi(\Phi(\delta(\mathsf{inr}\,{x})))\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=\Psi(\lambda\kappa.\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa{\Phi^{\kappa}(x)}))\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa{\Psi(\Phi(x))\,[\kappa]})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa{x\,[\kappa]})

which up to the isomorphism (
15
) is
\delta(\mathsf{inr}\,{x})
.
∎

Lemma A.16.

If
M:\mathsf{Tm}^{\Gamma}_{\mathsf{\sigma}}
and
\rho{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\Gamma}\,{}\delta
, then
\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho}{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{\sigma}\,{}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}
.

Here
\rho
is as usual,
\delta
is a mapping of variables
x:\tau
in
\Gamma
to
\delta(x):\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}
and
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}\triangleq\lambda\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta\,[\kappa]}
,
where
\delta\,[\kappa]
is pointwise application to
\kappa
. We have left the isomorphism of Lemma
A.15
implicit in the expression
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}
.

Before proving Lemma
A.16
, we prove a lemma that relates the
>\!>\!=
of
\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}
to the
>\!>\!=^{\kappa}
for
\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}
as it is used in the definition of
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}
.
This will allow us to use the bind lemma in the proof.

Lemma A.17.

Let
g:\forall\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}\to\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})
and
x:\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})
, and let
\Psi:\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})\to\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}})

be the isomorphism of Lemma
A.15
. Then

(\Psi(x)>\!>\!=z.\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa](z\,[\kappa]))=\lambda\kappa.(x\,[\kappa])>\!>\!=^{\kappa}g\,[\kappa]

both sides of type
\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\tau}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})
.

Proof.

First recall that
>\!>\!=
is defined in terms of
>\!>\!=^{\kappa}
. Precisely, given
x:\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}X
,
f:X\to\mathsf{D}^{\!\forall\!}Y
,

\displaystyle(x>\!>\!=f)=\lambda\kappa.(x\,[\kappa])>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda z.(f(z)\,[\kappa])

We prove that

(\Psi(x)>\!>\!=z.\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa](z\,[\kappa]))\,[\kappa]=(x\,[\kappa])>\!>\!=^{\kappa}g\,[\kappa]

by guarded recursion. Since
\mathcal{D}
commutes with
\forall\kappa
,
x
is on one of the following three forms

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle=\lambda\kappa.\delta(\mathsf{inl}(x^{\prime}\,[\kappa]))

\displaystyle\text{some }x^{\prime}:\forall\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle=\lambda\kappa.\delta(\mathsf{inr}(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}(x^{\prime}\,[\kappa]))))

\displaystyle\text{some }x^{\prime}:\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle=\lambda\kappa.{y\,[\kappa]}\oplus_{p}{z\,[\kappa]}

\displaystyle\text{some }z,y:\forall\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\sigma}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})

In the first case both sides reduce to
g\,[\kappa](x^{\prime}\,[\kappa])
. In the second case we get

\displaystyle(\Psi(x)>\!>\!=z.\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa](z\,[\kappa]))\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=(\Psi(x)\,[\kappa]>\!>\!=^{\kappa}z.g\,[\kappa](z\,[\kappa]))

\displaystyle=(\delta(\mathsf{inr}(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}((\Psi(x^{\prime}))\,[\kappa]))))>\!>\!=^{\kappa}z.g\,[\kappa](z\,[\kappa]))

\displaystyle=\delta(\mathsf{inr}(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}((\Psi(x^{\prime})\,[\kappa]>\!>\!=^{\kappa}z.g\,[\kappa](z\,[\kappa]))))))

\displaystyle=\delta(\mathsf{inr}(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}((\Psi(x^{\prime})>\!>\!=z.\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa](z\,[\kappa]))\,[\kappa]))))

\displaystyle=\delta(\mathsf{inr}(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}(x^{\prime}\,[\kappa])>\!>\!=^{\kappa}g\,[\kappa])))

\displaystyle=\delta(\mathsf{inr}(\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}(x^{\prime}\,[\kappa]))))>\!>\!=^{\kappa}g\,[\kappa]

\displaystyle=x\,[\kappa]>\!>\!=^{\kappa}g\,[\kappa]

We omit the last easy case.
∎

Proof of Lemma
A.16
.

The proof is by induction on the typing derivation of
M
.
Most cases are trivial, we just do a few.

Case
M=N\,P
. If
f{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma\to\tau}\,{}g
and
v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma}\,{}w
, then by definition
f\,v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{\tau}\,{}\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa]\,(w\,[\kappa])
. So, then also

\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa(f\,v)){}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{\tau}\,{}\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa]\,(w\,[\kappa])

Now, by Lemma
A.17
,

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N\,P$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}

\displaystyle=\lambda\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N\,P$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta\,[\kappa]}

\displaystyle=\lambda\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta\,[\kappa]}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}g.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle P$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta\,[\kappa]}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda w.gw

\displaystyle=\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}>\!>\!=g.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle P$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}>\!>\!=w.\lambda\kappa.g\,[\kappa](w\,[\kappa])

The case now follows by the bind lemma and the induction hypothesis.

Case
M=\mathsf{fold}{\,N}
. By induction
\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho}{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{\tau[\mu{X}.{\tau}/X]}\,{}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}
. We must show that

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}{(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa})}(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho}){}\,{}\overline{{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\mu{X}.{\tau}}\,{}}^{\kappa}\,{}\lambda\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}{(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa})}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta\,[\kappa]})

Suppose first that
v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau[\mu{X}.{\tau}/X]}\,{}w
. Then

\displaystyle(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{{\mu{X}.{\tau}}}\,{}\lambda\kappa.(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}{w\,[\kappa]}))

\displaystyle\leftrightarrow\triangleright^{\kappa}(v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau[\mu{X}.{\tau}/X]}\,{}w)

because
\lambda\kappa.(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}{w\,[\kappa]})\leadsto w
, and so is true. Note that

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}{(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa})}(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho})

\displaystyle=\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}v.\delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}v)

\displaystyle\lambda\kappa.\mathsf{D}^{\kappa}{(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa})}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta\,[\kappa]})

\displaystyle=\lambda\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta\,[\kappa]}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}w.\delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}w)

\displaystyle=\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}>\!>\!=w.\delta^{\forall\!}(\lambda\kappa.(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}(w\,[\kappa])))

using Lemma
A.17
in the last of these, so that the case now follows from an application of the bind lemma.

Case
M=\mathsf{unfold}{\,N}
. By induction
\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho}{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{\mu{X}.{\tau}}\,{}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}
. We must show that

\displaystyle\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda v.\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa\delta^{\kappa}{(v\,[\alpha])}){}\,{}\overline{{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau[\mu{X}.{\tau}/X]}\,{}}^{\kappa}\,{}\lambda\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta\,[\kappa]}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda w.\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa\delta^{\kappa}{(w\,[\alpha])})

Note first that by Lemma
A.17
,

\lambda\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\delta}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\lambda w.\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa\delta^{\kappa}{(w\,[\alpha])})=\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\delta}>\!>\!=\lambda w.\mathsf{step}^{\!\forall\!}(\delta^{\forall\!}w)

so by the bind lemma, the proof obligation reduces to showing

(16)

\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}(\char 12\relax\alpha\kappa\delta^{\kappa}{(v\,[\alpha])}){}\,{}\overline{{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau[\mu{X}.{\tau}/X]}\,{}}^{\kappa}\,{}\mathsf{step}^{\!\forall\!}(\delta^{\forall\!}w)

under the assumption that
v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\mu{X}.{\tau}}\,{}w
. By definition, the assumption means

{\triangleright}\,(\alpha\!:\!\kappa).(v\,[\alpha]{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau[\mu{X}.{\tau}/X]}\,{}\mathsf{force}(w))

and this is equivalent to (
16
) because
\mathsf{step}^{\!\forall\!}(\delta^{\forall\!}w)\leadsto\mathsf{force}(w)
.

In the case of
M=\mathsf{case}(L,x.N,y.P)
, note that

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{case}(L,x.N,y.P)$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\rho}

\displaystyle\triangleq\lambda\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle L$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{\rho\,[\kappa]}>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\begin{cases}{{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{w}\mapsto{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{(\rho\,[\kappa]).x\mapsto w}}\\
{{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{w}\mapsto{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle P$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}_{(\rho\,[\kappa]).y\mapsto w}}\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle L$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\rho}>\!>\!=\begin{cases}{{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{w}\mapsto{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\rho.x\mapsto w}}\\
{{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{w}\mapsto{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle P$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\rho.y\mapsto w}}\end{cases}

so that by the induction hypothesis and the bind lemma, the proof obligation reduces to showing

\left(\delta^{\kappa}v>\!>\!=^{\kappa}\begin{cases}{{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{v}\mapsto\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}{}(\lambda\alpha.{\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho.x\mapsto v}})\\
{{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{v}\mapsto\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}{}(\lambda\alpha.{\llparenthesis{P}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho.y\mapsto v}})\end{cases}\right){}\,{}\overline{{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau}\,{}}^{\kappa}\,{}\left(\delta^{\forall\!}w>\!>\!=\begin{cases}{{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{w}\mapsto{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\rho.x\mapsto w}}\\
{{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{w}\mapsto{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle P$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\rho.y\mapsto w}}\end{cases}\right)

assuming
v{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{{\sigma_{1}}+{\sigma_{2}}}\,{}w
. There are two cases for the latter, and we just show
the case of
v={{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{v^{\prime}}
and
w={{\smash{\mathbf{inl}}}}\,{w^{\prime}}
with
v^{\prime}{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\sigma_{1}}\,{}w^{\prime}
. In that case the obligation reduces to

\mathsf{step}^{\kappa}{}(\lambda\alpha.{\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho.x\mapsto v^{\prime}}}){}\,{}\overline{{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau}\,{}}^{\kappa}\,{}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\rho.x\mapsto w^{\prime}}}

which by definition is

\triangleright^{\kappa}(\llparenthesis{N}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}_{\rho.x\mapsto v^{\prime}}{}\,{}\overline{{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa,{\sf Val}}_{\tau}\,{}}^{\kappa}\,{}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle N$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}_{\rho.x\mapsto w^{\prime}}})

which is just
\triangleright^{\kappa}
applied to the induction hypothesis for
N
.
∎

We can now finally prove Theorem
6.1
.

Proof of Theorem
6.1
.

One direction follows directly from Lemma
8.3
: Since
\forall\kappa.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}{}\,\preceq^{\kappa,{\sf Tm}}_{1}\,{}\mathsf{eval}{\,M}
, by
Lemma
7.4
also
\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}})\leq_{\mathrm{lim}}\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\mathsf{eval}\,M)
. For the other direction,

\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\mathsf{eval}\,M)=\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis{})
by Corollary
A.14
, and since

\forall\kappa.(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis^{\kappa}{}\,\lesssim^{\kappa}_{1}\,{}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}{})
also
\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\llparenthesis{M}\rrparenthesis)\leq_{\mathrm{lim}}\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}{})
by Lemma
7.4
,
so
\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\mathsf{eval}\,M)\leq_{\mathrm{lim}}\mathsf{PT}_{(.)}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle M$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}{})
as required.
∎

Section
9

Proof of Theorem
9.3
.

Let
V_{{\sf R}_{1}}
,
V_{{\sf R}_{2}}
and
V_{\mathsf{2nd}}
be the values that

{\sf R}_{1}
,
{\sf R}_{2}
and
\mathsf{2nd}
reduce to. It suffices to show that
V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))\equiv_{\mathsf{Ctx}}V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n})
.
We first show
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}{}\,\preceq^{\kappa,{\sf Tm}}_{\mathsf{LazyL}}\,{}\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n}))
by guarded recursion, focussing just on the case of

n>0
, which is the harder one. Now,

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}

\displaystyle=\Delta^{\kappa}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle G_{{\sf R}_{1}}\,V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}

\displaystyle=\Delta^{\kappa}(\delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}({{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\underline{2n}},{\lambda\_.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!1}),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!+\!1)})}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}})))

\displaystyle=\Delta^{\kappa}\left(\delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}({{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\underline{2n}},{\lambda\_.{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!1})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!+\!1})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}))\right)

So that

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}

\displaystyle=\Delta^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle G_{\mathsf{2nd}}(\mathsf{Y}{\,G_{\mathsf{2nd}}})(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})

\displaystyle=\Delta^{\kappa}(\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle G_{\mathsf{2nd}}\,V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa})

\displaystyle=(\Delta^{\kappa})^{3}(\delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}({{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\underline{2n}},{\lambda\_.W_{{\sf R}_{1}}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}})))

where

W_{{\sf R}_{1}}=\left({\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(\mathsf{tl}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!1})))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(\mathsf{tl}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!+\!1})))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\right)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n}))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{eval}({\underline{2n}}::{\mathsf{lam}\ {y}.{\mathsf{ifz}(\underline{2n},\mathsf{nil},\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n}),\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n-2}),V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n+2}))))}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{fold}{\,(\mathsf{inr}{\,(}\underline{2n},\mathsf{lam}\ {y}.{\mathsf{ifz}(\underline{2n},\mathsf{nil},\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n}),\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2}),V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2}))))}))}

So showing
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}{}\,\preceq^{\kappa,{\sf Tm}}_{\mathsf{LazyL}}\,{}\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n}))
easily reduces to showing

(17)

\triangleright^{\kappa}(W_{{\sf R}_{1}}{}\,\preceq^{\kappa,{\sf Tm}}_{\mathsf{LazyL}}\,{}\mathsf{eval}(\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n}),\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2}),V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2})))))

Since
2n-1>0
, a similar reduction to above shows that

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(\mathsf{tl}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!1})))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}

\displaystyle=(\Delta^{\kappa})^{2}\left({\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\right)

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(\mathsf{tl}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!+\!1})))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}

\displaystyle=(\Delta^{\kappa})^{2}\left({\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\right)

so by Lemma
7.7
(
17
) is equivalent to

(\triangleright^{\kappa})^{3}\left(\begin{aligned} &{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\left({\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2}))$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\right)}\\
&{}\,\preceq^{\kappa,{\sf Tm}}_{\mathsf{LazyL}}\,{}{\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n}))}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\left({\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2}))}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2}))}\right)}\end{aligned}\right)

which follows from the guarded induction hypothesis and Lemma
7.8
.

For the other direction, we will show
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}{}\,\preceq^{\kappa,{\sf Tm}}_{\mathsf{LazyL}}\,{}\mathsf{eval}({V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))})
by guarded recursion, focussing
again on the case of
n>0
. First unfold definitions on the left hand side:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}

\displaystyle=\Delta^{\kappa}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle G_{{\sf R}_{2}}\,V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}

\displaystyle=\Delta^{\kappa}(\delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}({{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\underline{2n}},{\lambda\_.\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n}),\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2)}),V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2)}))}$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}})))

\displaystyle=\Delta^{\kappa}\left(\delta^{\kappa}(\mathsf{next}^{\kappa}({{\smash{\mathbf{inr}}}}\,{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle{\underline{2n}},{\lambda\_.{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\left({\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2)})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{2}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2)})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\right)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}))\right)

and on the right:

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{eval}(G_{{\sf R}_{1}}\,V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))

\displaystyle\leadsto{\underline{2n}}::{\mathsf{lam}\ {y}.{\mathsf{ifz}(\underline{2n},\mathsf{nil},\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!-\!1),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!+\!1)))}}

so that

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}({V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))})

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{eval}(G_{\mathsf{2nd}}\,\mathsf{2nd}\,(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n})))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{eval}(G_{\mathsf{2nd}}\,V_{\mathsf{2nd}}\,(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n})))

\displaystyle\leadsto{\underline{2n}}::{\mathsf{lam}\ {y}.{V_{\mathsf{2nd}}}(\mathsf{tl}(\mathsf{lam}\ {y}.{\mathsf{ifz}(\underline{2n},\mathsf{nil},\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!-\!1),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!+\!1)))}\,\langle\rangle))}

and so the goal reduces to showing

(18)

\triangleright^{\kappa}\left(\begin{aligned} &{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\left({\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2(n\!-\!1)})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\oplus^{\kappa}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\llbracket}\hbox{$\SavedStyle V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2(n\!+\!1)})$}\mathclose{}{\rrbracket}}^{\kappa}}\right)}{}\,\preceq^{\kappa,{\sf Tm}}_{\mathsf{LazyL}}\,{}\\
&\mathsf{eval}(V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(\mathsf{tl}(\mathsf{lam}\ {y}.{\mathsf{ifz}(\underline{2n},\mathsf{nil},\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!-\!1),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!+\!1)))}\,\langle\rangle)))\end{aligned}\right)

Now,

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}(\mathsf{tl}(\mathsf{lam}\ {y}.{\mathsf{ifz}(\underline{2n},\mathsf{nil},\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!-\!1),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!+\!1)))}\,\langle\rangle))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{eval}(\mathsf{tl}(\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!-\!1),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!+\!1))))

\displaystyle\leadsto{\mathsf{eval}(\mathsf{tl}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!-\!1)))}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathsf{eval}(\mathsf{tl}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!+\!1)))}

\displaystyle\leadsto{\mathsf{eval}(\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2}),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n})))}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathsf{eval}(\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2})))}

\displaystyle={\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}))}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\left({\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2}))}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathsf{eval}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2}))}\right)}

so that

\displaystyle\mathsf{eval}(V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(\mathsf{tl}(\mathsf{lam}\ {y}.{\mathsf{ifz}(\underline{2n},\mathsf{nil},\mathsf{choice}^{\frac{1}{2}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!-\!1),V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n}\!+\!1)))}\,\langle\rangle)))

\displaystyle\leadsto{\mathsf{eval}(V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n})))}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\left({\mathsf{eval}(V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!-\!2})))}\oplus^{\!\forall\!}_{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathsf{eval}(V_{\mathsf{2nd}}(V_{{\sf R}_{1}}(\underline{2n\!+\!2})))}\right)}

From this it follows that (
18
) can be proved using the guarded recursion assumption.
∎
